I couldn't watch the recording, though: `/app/.harbor_assets/` is empty and there's no `recording.mp4` anywhere on the system. So I worked from the written description, and I haven't run the game to confirm the ship now stays at 100%.\n\n**Cause:** the Skipjack's low-detail model file, `data/models/ships/skipjack/skipjack_low.dae`, put the landing reference point (`tag_landing`) at y = −3.861. The high- and medium-detail files both use −4.311, which lines up with the bottom of the landing gear and the bottom of the collision mesh. The game loads detail levels from lowest to highest and keeps the first `tag_landing` it finds, so it used the wrong value.\n\n**Effect:** a docked Skipjack was placed 0.45 m too low, with its collision outline sunk into the pad even though the model looks like it's resting clear. When it takes off, collision switches back on while the ship is already inside the pad. By then the pad is no longer reserved for it, so the hit counts as an ordinary crash and damages the hull. Every pad on every station places ships the same way, so all pads were affected.\n\n**Fix:** in `skipjack_low.dae` (line 871) I changed the tag's height from −3.860857 to −4.310857, matching the other two detail levels. That puts the bottom of the collision outline exactly at pad level when docked.\n\nWhat this leaves alone:\n- **Ship shape:** the collision mesh and visual geometry are untouched, so the ship's shape stays the same.\n- **Pad collision:** pad and docking collision code are unchanged, so a ship that really lands on a pad is still caught.\n\n**How I checked other ships:** I wrote a small script that compares every ship's `tag_landing`, in each detail level, against the bottom of its collision mesh. Skipjack was the only real mismatch. Pumpkinseed and Sinonatrix differ by 4–6 mm, which looks like normal modelling tolerance, so I left them alone."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":507,"cache_read_input_tokens":69131,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":507,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b80c0c94-b603-4589-8f23-8a31dc4bf086","uuid":"4d083cc4-6c9b-4b0f-b6aa-3a6bfcb5da76","timestamp":"2026-11-15T18:32:10.536Z"}
